# Full LBT and colmat-rs routine mapping

## Scope and classification

Lists **all 5,146 canonical symbols** exposed by LBT in Julia 1.13.4.
LP64 / ILP64 are separate tables with the same canonical names. Types and ABIs are not omitted to claim blanket support.
The colmat-rs target is `4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82` in `deps/upstream.toml`.
This is a static audit of connection candidates based on the public source. It is not the result of executing every candidate below.
Results of actual Julia calls are recorded separately in [Measured Julia API mapping](@ref).

- **Public numerical capability available**: Numerical kernels such as LU, SVD, and eigenvalue decomposition are public. This does not mean the ABI is identical.
- **Storage conversion required**: Differences exist in reflectors, permutations, band/packed storage, and so on. An adapter is not necessarily available yet.
- **Composition candidate**: A candidate whose mathematical output can be produced by combining public kernels. Equivalence in LAPACK-specific algorithms, accuracy guarantees, or complexity is not claimed.
- **Partial**: Basic factorizations exist, but condition estimates, fixed pivots, expert flags, and similar features are missing.
- **No independent public API (within reviewed scope)**: A determination within the recorded scope of revisions, types, storage formats, options, and semantic verification methods. It does not mean the mathematics cannot be implemented.
- **Unverified; insufficient evidence**: Unregistered families, guesses based on names, and old unsupported classifications with insufficient confirmation records. Does not mean the public API is absent.
- `capability_status` and `capability_evidence` record the public-feature assessment; `adapter_lp64/ilp64` records the actual ABI connection; `execution_evidence_status` records the registration state of per-entry execution evidence; and `independent_algorithm_status` records verification of independent algorithms, all separately. `not_catalogued` does not mean untested. Do not infer the presence of an independent algorithm from `not_assessed`.
- `adapter` is the actual LBT registration. Do not confuse the existence of a numerical feature, the ABI connection, and completed verification.

## Machine-readable list of all symbols

[The all-symbol TSV](../assets/lbt-symbol-inventory.tsv) contains **one row per symbol**, with the name, C/Fortran ABI, type, public-feature classification, LP64/ILP64 connection status, source evidence, and restrictions.
[The generation-metadata JSON](../assets/lbt-symbol-inventory.json) is also saved.

| ABI | Canonical symbols |
|---|---|
| LAPACKE | 2,605 |
| Fortran | 2,329 |
| CBLAS | 212 |

The current adapter registers LP64 **3116** and ILP64 **3116** symbols.
GEEVX condition estimation is delegated to the existing backend rather than the native one. This is not a change that provides all of LAPACKE.

## LAPACK connected in this round

| Family | Numerical processing | Restrictions |
|---|---|---|
| GEQRT / GEQRT2 / GEQRT3 / GEMQRT | Native QR coefficient conversion and block reflector application | Allocates internal arrays. GEQRT3 uses the common path |
| GETRF / GETRF2 / GETF2 / GETRS / GETRI / GESV | colmat-rs LU and triangular solve | Converts the final permutation to IPIV |
| POTRF / POTRF2 / POTF2 / POTRS / POTRI / POSV | colmat-rs LLT and triangular solve | Converts UPLO and INFO |
| GESDD / GESVD | colmat-rs SVD | Converts full/thin/overwrite and VT. Both drivers connect to the common native SVD |
| GEEV / GEEVX | colmat-rs balancing, EVD, and Schur | GEEVX uses native SENSE=N. Expert modes use the original implementation; LP64 converts integers to ILP64 as needed. Requires an ILP64 implementation before registration |
| GGEV / GGEV3 / GEGV / GGEVX | colmat-rs GEVD, homogeneous alpha/beta, and left/right eigenvectors | GGEVX uses native BALANC=N/SENSE=N. Other modes use the existing ILP64 backend with integer ABI conversion |
| SYEV / HEEV / SYEVD / HEEVD | colmat-rs self-adjoint EVD | No guarantee of running the same Netlib algorithm as the driver name |
| LARFG / LARFGP / LARF / LARFX / LARFY / LARFT / LARFB | Rust reflector generation and native GEMV/SYMV/GEMM | Converts LAPACK coefficients, F/B, and C/R. LARFB allocates internal arrays |
| GGQRF / GGRQF / GGLSE / GGGLM | native-backed QR/RQ, Q application, and triangular solve composition | External reflector compatibility, equality constraints, minimum norm, and INFO=1/2 for exact rank failure. Allocates internal coefficients and scratch |
| TREXC / LAEXC / TRSEN / GEESX | Native checked Schur moves and the existing GEES | Condition estimation uses the saved original ILP64 routines. Converts rejection indices, the total selected SDIM, integer work arrays, and the callback ABI |
| TREVC / TREVC3 | Public Schur eigenvectors, reversed adjoint, and back-transformation | SIDE L/R/B, HOWMNY A/B/S, SELECT/M for real pairs, and max-component normalization. Native rejections use the saved original LAPACK |
| LACPY / LASET / LASWP | Native LACPY/LASET/LASWP and direct row-major conversion | Reads and writes only the logical rectangle and selected triangle. LASWP swaps whole rows and preserves Fortran ordering for INCX<0 |
| TGEVC | Eigenvector back-substitution from an existing S/P Schur pencil and Q/Z back-transformation | Ports LAPACK's guarded small solves to Rust. SIDE L/R/B, HOWMNY A/S/B, real conjugate pairs, infinite eigenvalues, and max-component normalization. C API is #288 |
| LANGE / LANSY / LANTR | Native matrix norms and row-major transpose views | M/1/O/I/F/E, symmetric/triangular storage, and the unit diagonal. Returns a real value, not INFO |
| GESVDQ | SVD with native QR preprocessing | Transposes A and writes it back (QR factors); U is M×M for JOBU=A, N×N for F, and M×N otherwise (R has NUMRANK columns); V is written back as the logical NUMRANK or N rows by N columns. High-level entry points query IWORK/WORK/RWORK |
| GGESX | Native generalized Schur decomposition (reordering and condition estimation) | Passes the SELECTCTG callback to native reordering and transposes A/B/VSL/VSR before writing everything back. Queries WORK/LWORK/LIWORK, and the high-level entry adds slack to LWORK for the TGSYL stage of SENSE |
| TGSEN | Native cluster reordering of a generalized Schur pair | SELECT is a LOGICAL (integer) array. Transposes A/B/Q/Z and writes everything back, querying WORK/LWORK/LIWORK. The TGSYL stage for IJOB>=1 needs at least one more LWORK element than the queried minimum, so the high-level entry adds slack |
| GESVDX | Native selected singular value decomposition | RANGE=A/V/I; row-major U is M×NS and VT is NS×N, writing back only the selected NS pairs. The high-level entry queries WORK/LWORK and uses SUPERB as integer workspace |
| BDSQR / BDSDC / BDSVDX / LASQ1 | Native bidiagonal SVD and selection | U/VT/C application, COMPQ N/I, RANGE A/V/I, and ascending output. The compact P of BDSDC is delegated to the saved original ILP64 LAPACK |
| LAPMR / LAPMT | Native row/column permutation | The row-major path converts only the logical rectangle. Preserves K and forward/backward |
| ORGTR/UNGTR and ORMTR/UNMTR | Native tridiagonal Q formation and application | The row-major path preserves the selected triangle and converts the logical rectangle. Converts UPLO/SIDE/TRANS and the lwork query |
| ORGBR/ORMBR and ORGHR/ORMHR | Native bidiagonal/Hessenberg Q formation and application | Converts VECT/SIDE/TRANS/ILO/IHI and the lwork query. The row-major path handles only the logical rectangle |
| TREVC | Native Schur eigenvectors | SIDE/HOWMNY/SELECT/M, real conjugate pairs, and row-major logical-rectangle conversion. C ABI is #288 |
| TREXC | Native Schur block move | COMPQ V/N, IFST/ILST, and the ABI difference between real pointers and complex by-value arguments. C ABI is #288 |
| LASCL | Native safe-range scaling | TYPE G/L/U/H/B/Q/Z, the number of row-major band rows, CFROM/CTO, and INFO. C ABI is #288 |
| LACGV / LACP2 | Native conjugation and real-to-complex copy | INCX and the selected triangle, plus row-major conversion from real A to complex B. LACGV has no layout argument |
| TRSEN | Native checked Schur selection (JOB=N) | SELECT/M, reordering, and row-major T/Q. JOB E/V/B use the saved original LAPACK |
| LARFG | Native Householder generator | No layout argument; converts INCX and TAU/alpha/x directly |
| LARFT | Native block reflector triangular factor | DIRECT/STOREV and row-major V/T conversion. Preserves the transpose convention of T |
| LARFB | Native block reflector application | SIDE/TRANS/DIRECT/STOREV and row-major V/T/C conversion. WORK length is K |
| LARTGP / LARTGS | Native Givens rotation with scalar guards | No layout argument; converts by-value f/g/sigma and the NaN ordering |
| GEES | Native Schur driver (SORT=N/S) | Passes the SELECT callback directly and converts row-major A/VS, WORK/BWORK, and SDIM |
| GEESX | Native/saved Schur expert driver | SENSE, RCONDE/RCONDV, WORK/IWORK/RWORK, and row-major A/VS |
| GGEV / GGEV3 | native GEVD driver | JOBVL/JOBVR、alpha/beta、row-major A/B/VL/VR、WORK/RWORK |
| GGEVX | Native/saved GEVD expert driver | BALANC/SENSE, condition numbers, ILO/IHI, IWORK/BWORK, and row-major A/B/VL/VR |
| GGHRD / GGHD3 | Native generalized Hessenberg reduction | COMPQ/COMPZ V/N, ILO/IHI, row-major A/B/Q/Z, and the GGHD3 work |
| HGEQZ | native generalized Schur driver (JOB=E) | COMPQ/COMPZ、alpha/beta、row-major H/T/Q/Z、WORK/RWORK |
| UPGTR / UPMTR | Native packed tridiagonal Q formation and application | C/Z only. Row-major packed AP reordering, SIDE/TRANS, and row-major C |
| OPGTR / OPMTR | Native packed tridiagonal Q formation and application | S/D only. Row-major packed AP reordering, SIDE/TRANS, and row-major C |
| PTTRF / PTTRS | Native positive tridiagonal factorization/solve | PTTRF without layout, row-major B for PTTRS, and real D with complex E for complex types |
| PBTRF / PBTRS | Native banded positive-definite factorization/solve | Row-major band AB (kd+1 rows × LDAB columns), row-major B, and LDAB/LDB conversion |
| SBTRD / HBTRD | Native banded tridiagonal reduction | Row-major band AB, Q for VECT=V, D/E, and LDAB/LDQ conversion |
| PTEQR | Native tridiagonal eigensolver | COMPZ N/V/I, row-major Z, real WORK of length 4N-4, and D/E |
| PPTRF / PPTRS | Native packed positive-definite factorization/solve | Row-major packed AP with pp_trans reordering, row-major B, and no LDAP |
| GBTRF / GBTRS / GBSV | Native general band LU | Row-major band AB (2KL+KU+1 rows × LDAB columns), IPIV, row-major B, and TRANS |
| GBEQU / GBEQUB | Native band equilibration | Analysis band AB (KL+KU+1 rows), R/C/ROWCND/COLCND/AMAX, and row-major conversion |
| GTTRF / GTTRS / GTSV | Native tridiagonal LU | GTTRF without layout, DU2/IPIV, TRANS, and row-major B |
| SPTRF / SPTRS / SPSV | Native packed Bunch-Kaufman LU | S/D only. Row-major packed AP with sp_trans reordering, IPIV, and row-major B |
| SYTRF/SYTRS/SYSV and HETRF/HETRS/HESV | Native dense Bunch-Kaufman LU | UPLO, IPIV, WORK query, and row-major A/B. SY uses S/D and HE uses C/Z |
| GEBAL / GEBAK | native balancing and back-transform | JOB N/P/S/B、ILO/IHI、real SCALE、row-major A/V、SIDE L/R |
| TBTRS and TPTRS/TPTRI | Native triangular band/packed solve and inverse | UPLO/TRANS/DIAG, row-major band AB and packed AP reordering, and row-major B |
| LANGB / LANHE | Native band and Hermitian norms | Row-major conversion of the analysis band AB (KL+KU+1 rows) and of the selected triangle. Row-major returns the same values as column-major |
| GEHRD / GEBRD / LARFX | Native Hessenberg reduction, bidiagonalization, and reflector application | ILO/IHI, D/E/TAUQ/TAUP, work queries, row-major A/C, and the unblocked LARFX path for small dimensions |
| GEEQU / GEEQUB | Native dense equilibration | R/C/ROWCND/COLCND/AMAX and row-major logical-rectangle conversion of A. GEEQUB scales by powers of two |
| GGES / GGES3 | Native generalized Schur driver | JOBVSL/JOBVR/SORT, alpha/beta, row-major A/B/VSL/VSR, and the GGES3 work query |
| HSEQR | native Hessenberg Schur decomposition | JOB E/S、COMPZ N/I/V、ILO/IHI、WR/WI vs W、row-major H/Z |
| GEQR/GELQ and GEMQR/GEMLQ | Native blocked QR/LQ interfaces | T/TSIZE descriptors, row-major A (R×K and K×R), row-major C, and work queries |
| PBEQU / PBSTF / PBSV | Native positive-definite band extras | S/SCOND/AMAX, the split Cholesky factor, row-major band AB (KD+1 rows), and B |
| PFTRF / PFTRI / PFTRS | Native RFP Cholesky | TRANSR/UPLO, transposition of the RFP rectangle (ROW×COL), and row-major B |
| TRTTF/TFTTR, TFTTP/TPTTF, TPTTR/TRTTP | Native full/RFP/packed conversion | Transposition of the RFP rectangle (dtf_trans), packed pp_trans reordering, and row-major A/LDA |
| BDSQR, BDSDC, BDSVDX | Native bidiagonal SVD | Transposes and writes back VT/U/C; BDSDC validates COMPQ=N/I (COMPQ=P is only passed to the native compact fallback); BDSVDX writes back only the columns returned in the 2N-row [U;V] Z |
| GETSLS, GETSQRHRT, ORGTSQR_ROW/UNGTSQR_ROW, ORHR_COL/UNHR_COL | Native tall-skinny least squares and TSQR Householder reconstruction | T is an NB×(N*number of slots) array and the row-major LDT must be at least the number of columns. GETSLS/GETSQRHRT/ORGTSQR_ROW query WORK/LWORK and transpose A/B/T before writing back |
| LAG2C/LAG2D/LAG2S/LAG2Z, LACRM/LARCM, PSTRF | Native precision conversion, complex×real matrix products, and pivoted Cholesky | Row-major conversion and write-back. LAG2* overflow reports a positive INFO, LACRM/LARCM have no INFO (column-major argument errors come only from the native checker), and PSTRF writes back only the triangle and passes through PIV/RANK |
| SYTRD/HETRD and SYEQUB/HEEQUB | Native dense tridiagonalization and diagonal scaling | NaN checks and write-back only on the UPLO triangle; SYTRD/HETRD query WORK/LWORK; SYEQUB/HEEQUB treat A as read-only and use CABS1 for complex types |
| STEVX/STEVR/STEGR/STEMR | Native selected tridiagonal eigenvalues | NaN checks only for D/E (N elements for E in STEMR); row-major Z only for returned columns; STEVR/STEGR/STEMR query WORK/IWORK; STEMR passes NZC/TRYRAC |
| **Not connected (no independent native public API): GEJSV/GESVJ** | None | The S/D/C/Z GEJSV and GESVJ (Jacobi SVD) have no numerical entry point on the colmat-rs/Colmat.jl side. Ordinary SVD is not substituted to make them succeed, and they are not registered with LBT (the standard LAPACK is left in charge). |
| **Not connected (different contract): complex symmetric CSY*/CSP*** and ZSY*/ZSP*** (SYTRF/SYTRS/SYSV/SYTRI/SYTRI2/SYTRI2X/SYTRS2, SPTRF/SPTRS/SPSV/SPTRI, and their ROOK variants) | None | Native Bunch-Kaufman has a Hermitian LDL^H contract, so the factors and IPIV of complex symmetric LDL^T are different. The Hermitian version is not substituted, and these are not registered with LBT (the standard LAPACK is left in charge). |
| **Unverified modes** | — | BDSDC COMPQ=P (compact form) only forwards to the native compact fallback. The row-major paths of BDSQR/BDSDC/BDSVDX verify COMPQ=N/I and all modes of BDSQR/BDSVDX. |
| SYEVX/HEEVX, SYGVX/HEGVX, SBGVX/HBGVX, SPGVX/HPGVX | Native selected eigenvalues (dense, band, and packed, including generalized) | NaN checks and write-back only on the UPLO triangle; row-major Z only for returned columns; SYEVX/SYGVX query WORK/LWORK; the band Q is transposed only for JOBZ=V |
| SBEVX/HBEVX, SPEVX/HPEVX, SPTRD/HPTRD | Native band/packed selected eigenvalues and packed tridiagonalization | Converts band AB and packed AP to column-major and writes back; row-major Q/Z only for JOBZ=V, and Z writes back only returned columns. WORK/IWORK(/RWORK) are allocated at fixed length |
| SBGST/HBGST/SPGST/HPGST, SBGV/HBGV/SPGV/HPGV, SBGVD/HBGVD/SPGVD/HPGVD | Native band/packed generalized self-adjoint | Converts band AB/BB (KA+1/KB+1 rows) and packed AP/BP to column-major and writes back, transposing row-major Z/X. GST reads the split or ordinary Cholesky factor of B read-only. GVD variants query WORK/IWORK(/RWORK) |
| SYGST/HEGST, SYGV/HEGV, SYGVD/HEGVD | Native generalized self-adjoint definite problems | ITYPE 1–3. NaN checks only on the UPLO triangle; row-major A is the full matrix for JOBZ=V and triangular otherwise; B's triangle is written back. GST reads B read-only. GVD variants query WORK/IWORK(/RWORK) |
| TGEXC | Native reordering of a generalized Schur pair (QZ reorder) | Real IFST/ILST are input/output pointers, while complex ones are passed by value. Transposes A/B/Q/Z and queries the native routine directly |
| SYTRF/SYTRS/SYSV_ROOK and HETRF/HETRS_ROOK | Native rook Bunch-Kaufman | Factors and IPIV (negative 2×2 pivots) are used interchangeably in row/column-major. Complex symmetric CSYTRF_ROOK and HESV_ROOK are out of scope |
| SFRK/HFRK | Native RFP rank-k update | alpha/beta are passed by value, and the RFP rectangle (ROW×COL) and row-major A are transposed. No INFO is returned, so column-major argument errors come only from the native checker |
| SBEV/HBEV/SPEV/HPEV and SBEVD/HBEVD/SPEVD/HPEVD | Native band/packed full eigen-decomposition | Converts band AB (KD+1 rows) and packed AP to column-major and transposes row-major Z. EVD variants allocate through WORK/IWORK(/RWORK) queries |
| STERF/STEQR/STEV/STEVD/STEDC | Native tridiagonal EVD (QL/QR, divide and conquer) | STERF has no matrix_layout. Row-major Z is read as input only for COMPZ=V, and STEDC/STEVD allocate through WORK/IWORK(/RWORK) queries |
| SPTRI, HPTRF/HPTRS/HPSV/HPTRI, SYTRS2/HETRS2 | Native packed Bunch-Kaufman and dense solves | Reorders packed AP with pp_trans and excludes the imaginary part of the Hermitian diagonal from NaN checks. SYTRS2 allocates N elements without using WORK |
| SYTRI/HETRI, SYTRI2/HETRI2, SYTRI2X/HETRI2X | Native Bunch-Kaufman inverse | Factors and IPIV are used interchangeably. The row-major path checks NaN and writes back only the UPLO triangle; TRI2 queries WORK; TRI2X uses a work area of (N+NB+1)*(NB+3) |
| POEQU/POEQUB, PPEQU, PPSV/PPTRI | Native positive-definite equilibration and packed extras | S/SCOND/AMAX (dense/packed), factor write-back for PPSV, and factor to inv(A) for PPTRI |
| TFSM / TFTRI and PTSV | Native RFP triangular operations and positive-definite tridiagonal solves | SIDE/UPLO/TRANS/DIAG, row-major RFP rectangle transposition and B, and D/E with row-major B for PTSV |
| TGEXC / TGEX2 / TGSEN / GGESX | Public QZ reorder and eigenvalue extraction from saved factors | Condition and separation estimates use the existing ILP64 LAPACK. Converts integer arrays and callback LOGICAL, and allocates internal matrices |
| SBTRD/HBTRD/SPTRD/HPTRD and OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR | Native tridiagonalization / Householder | Band/packed expansion, external AP/TAU interoperability, and the X*Q update for VECT=U. Not a dedicated band bulge chase |
| GST/GV/GVD/GVX for SBG/HBG/SPG/HPG | Native triangular/split Cholesky, congruence transformation, and full EVD | Converts packed ITYPE and the band split factor. The GVX Q is the full generalized eigenbasis, and positive ABSTOL and boundary guards use the saved original LAPACK |
| SYEVR / HEEVR | Full EVD and A/I/V range selection | Does not provide native MRRR / Sturm count |

## Full list of connection candidates and unsupported routines

The following is grouped by family. For each row, all applicable symbols are listed explicitly. Types are kept in the TSV as `s/d/c/z`, and everything else as `other`.
Evidence links point to the public source at the pinned revision. Restrictions within a row do not mean that they have been verified to satisfy every LAPACK contract.

### Public numerical capability available

| Family / ABI | All symbols | Public feature and evidence | Contract differences |
|---|---|---|---|
| `gemm` / Fortran | `cgemm_`, `dgemm_`, `sgemm_`, `zgemm_` | [matmul / triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Converts shapes, triangular parts, conjugates, and coefficients. |
| `gemm` / CBLAS | `cblas_cgemm`, `cblas_dgemm`, `cblas_sgemm`, `cblas_zgemm` | [matmul / triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Converts shapes, triangular parts, conjugates, and coefficients.; C ABI connected for LP64/ILP64 with row/column-major and explicit enum/coefficient/return conversions; #271 tests DLL and LBT routes |
| `gemm3m` / Fortran | `cgemm3m_`, `zgemm3m_` | [matmul / triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Fortran GEMMT/GEMMTR write only the selected triangle; SYR2/HER2 compose native rank products with signed strides and ignore the old Hermitian diagonal imaginary component. ### Supported; storage conversion required

| Family / ABI | All symbols | Public feature and evidence | Contract differences |
|---|---|---|---|
| `gbmv` / Fortran | `cgbmv_`, `dgbmv_`, `sgbmv_`, `zgbmv_` | [native interval matmul / packed outer products / triangular_solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Connects 44 Fortran BLAS symbols to both ABIs. Passes only the stored band/packed interval to native multiplication and distinguishes negative strides, complex symmetric versus Hermitian, real diagonals, and the unit diagonal. Triangular solves compose the interval's native dot product and a diagonal solve, and zero columns in the no-transpose case are skipped as in the reference Netlib. No full dense expansion; allocates internal storage for the X snapshot of the product and the necessary row gathers. C ABI is a separate task. |
| `gbmv` / CBLAS | `cblas_cgbmv`, `cblas_dgbmv`, `cblas_sgbmv`, `cblas_zgbmv` | [native interval matmul / packed outer products / triangular_solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Connects 44 Fortran BLAS symbols to both ABIs. GTSV also handles its own DL contract, stopping at the first singular pivot and preserving the partial B and second superdiagonal. No full dense expansion. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `gebd2` / Fortran | `cgebd2_`, `dgebd2_`, `sgebd2_`, `zgebd2_` | [native bidiag panels / LAPACK reflector gauge / native GEMV updates](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/svd/bidiag.rs) | Connects 20 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Normalizes each active column/row, uses the single-column panel of native bidiag_in_place, and converts complex beta and the reciprocal-coefficient convention to LAPACK's real D/E, complex TAUQ/TAUP, and stored vectors. GEBRD/GEBD2 share the unblocked composition. LABRD updates its owned working matrix in sequence, preserving caller A22 while returning the first NB rows/columns and the deferred X/Y. Q/P are generated and applied left/right from the same storage form with native GEMV and rank-one updates. Handles the minimum length and query of caller WORK but allocates internal storage separately. Verifies four types, both ABIs, upper/lower bidiagonal form, rectangular shapes, and interoperability with external factors. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `gtsv` / Fortran | `cgtsv_`, `dgtsv_`, `sgtsv_`, `zgtsv_` | [native column PartialPivLu / rank-one matmul / interval triangular_solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/lu/partial_pivoting/factor.rs) | Connects 32 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Factors the stored band columns with native LU and preserves LAPACK's interleaved IPIV and fill-in through row exchanges and skew-strided outer-product updates. Converts tridiagonal systems to an O(n) four-row band region and reads/writes DL/D/DU/DU2 and adjacent pivots. Supports N/T/C solves from external factors. GTSV also handles its own DL contract, stopping at the first singular pivot and preserving the partial B and second superdiagonal. No full dense expansion. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `hbev` / Fortran | `chbev_`, `zhbev_` | [dense expansion / native full-spectrum self-adjoint EVD](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects 24 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Expands the specified band/packed interval into an owned O(n^2) Hermitian matrix and uses the native full EVD. BEV/BEVD and PEV/PEVD share a path and do not claim dedicated band/packed algorithms. VX selects A/I/V from the full spectrum, band Q returns all eigenvectors, and AB returns the diagonalized tridiagonal matrix. Positive ABSTOL, native failures, and range endpoints inside the precision-based guard are passed to the saved ILP64 expert driver, converting LP64 integer arrays. Preserves the workspace query and minimum legal work but allocates the native internal region separately. C ABI is a separate task. |

Passes only the stored band/packed interval to native multiplication and distinguishes negative strides, complex symmetric versus Hermitian, real diagonals, and the unit diagonal. Triangular solves compose the interval's native dot product and a diagonal solve, and zero columns in the no-transpose case are skipped as in the reference Netlib. No full dense expansion; allocates internal storage for the X snapshot of the product and the necessary row gathers. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `hbtrd` / Fortran | `chbtrd_`, `zhbtrd_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

Symmetric congruence scaling for subnormal rows and common scaling for D/RHS blocks avoid native #212. LAPACKE waits for the shared conversion in #288. No claim of the same algorithm, pivot columns, or performance as the dedicated Fortran routines. Complex symmetric SY has a separate contract. |

Symmetric congruence scaling for subnormal rows and common scaling for D/RHS blocks avoid native #212. LAPACKE waits for the shared conversion in #288. No claim of the same algorithm, pivot columns, or performance as the dedicated Fortran routines. Complex symmetric SY has a separate contract. |

| `hfrk` / Fortran | `chfrk_`, `zhfrk_` | [RFP selected-triangle conversion / native triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/triangular.rs) | Connects the four Fortran entry points SSFRK/DSFRK/CHFRK/ZHFRK to both ABIs. Converts RFP odd/even, UPLO, real N/T versus complex N/C, and rank-k transpose. Allocates an O(n^2) internal native matrix. beta=0 uses the old C, and a zero product does not read A. | `hpev` / Fortran | `chpev_`, `zhpev_` | [dense expansion / native full-spectrum self-adjoint EVD](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects 24 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Expands the specified band/packed interval into an owned O(n^2) Hermitian matrix and uses the native full EVD. BEV/BEVD and PEV/PEVD share a path and do not claim dedicated band/packed algorithms. VX selects A/I/V from the full spectrum, band Q returns all eigenvectors, and AB returns the diagonalized tridiagonal matrix. Positive ABSTOL, native failures, and range endpoints inside the precision-based guard are passed to the saved ILP64 expert driver, converting LP64 integer arrays. Preserves the workspace query and minimum legal work but allocates the native internal region separately. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `hpgst` / Fortran | `chpgst_`, `zhpgst_` | [factorized triangular/split B + native triangular_solve / matmul / reflectors](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/triangular_solve.rs) | Connects eight Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Packed GST reads an external triangular Cholesky factor and applies the congruence transformation for ITYPE=1/2/3. Band GST reads the PBSTF split factor S and, through native block triangular solves and reflector composition, forms C=X^H A X and X^H B X=I within the original bandwidth. Preserves the unspecified triangle, padding, and BB, and reads only the real part of the complex Hermitian diagonal. Allocates dense internal matrices and does not claim the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. Band native rejections use the saved ILP64 driver. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `hpgv` / Fortran | `chpgv_`, `zhpgv_` | [native LLT / split-factor solves / self_adjoint_evd](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects 24 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Packed input stores a triangular Cholesky factor and band input the PBSTF split factor; composes the congruence transformation, native full EVD, and B normalization. GVD also uses the common native EVD and does not claim the identically named divide-and-conquer algorithm. GVX selects A/I/V; the band full-basis Q returns generalized eigenvectors, and AB returns the diagonal tridiagonal form. Positive ABSTOL, native failures, and precision-guarded range endpoints are delegated to the saved original ILP64 driver, converting only written LP64 IWORK/IFAIL slots. Allocates an O(n^2) owned matrix and scratch. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `hptrd` / Fortran | `chptrd_`, `zhptrd_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `labrd` / Fortran | `clabrd_`, `dlabrd_`, `slabrd_`, `zlabrd_` | [native bidiag panels / LAPACK reflector gauge / native GEMV updates](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/svd/bidiag.rs) | Connects 20 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Normalizes each active column/row, uses the single-column panel of native bidiag_in_place, and converts complex beta and the reciprocal-coefficient convention to LAPACK's real D/E, complex TAUQ/TAUP, and stored vectors. GEBRD/GEBD2 share the unblocked composition. LABRD updates its owned working matrix in sequence, preserving caller A22 while returning the first NB rows/columns and the deferred X/Y. Q/P are generated and applied left/right from the same storage form with native GEMV and rank-one updates. Handles the minimum length and query of caller WORK but allocates internal storage separately. Verifies four types, both ABIs, upper/lower bidiagonal form, rectangular shapes, and interoperability with external factors. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `opgtr` / Fortran | `dopgtr_`, `sopgtr_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `org2r` / Fortran | `dorg2r_`, `sorg2r_` | [qr_in_place / Householder / triangular solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Connects 36 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Provides A/TAU coefficient conversion, Q formation and left/right application, and GEQRS. The complex ordinary and nonnegative-diagonal variants generate LAPACK-gauge reflectors and preserve real beta. Allocates internal arrays, and no guarantee is made that each entry point uses the same Netlib implementation as its algorithm name. |
| `orgbr` / Fortran | `dorgbr_`, `sorgbr_` | [native bidiag panels / LAPACK reflector gauge / native GEMV updates](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/svd/bidiag.rs) | Connects 20 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Normalizes each active column/row, uses the single-column panel of native bidiag_in_place, and converts complex beta and the reciprocal-coefficient convention to LAPACK's real D/E, complex TAUQ/TAUP, and stored vectors. GEBRD/GEBD2 share the unblocked composition. LABRD updates its owned working matrix in sequence, preserving caller A22 while returning the first NB rows/columns and the deferred X/Y. Q/P are generated and applied left/right from the same storage form with native GEMV and rank-one updates. Handles the minimum length and query of caller WORK but allocates internal storage separately. Verifies four types, both ABIs, upper/lower bidiagonal form, rectangular shapes, and interoperability with external factors. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `pbstf` / Fortran | `cpbstf_`, `dpbstf_`, `spbstf_`, `zpbstf_` | [native diagonal Llt / triangular_solve / triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/cholesky/llt/factor.rs) | Connects four Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Composes split Cholesky by eliminating the second half in reverse order, updating the first half, and then factorizing the first half forward. This is not a replacement by ordinary POTRF; A=S^H*S is reconstructed independently from the upper, lower, and crossing parts of S. The split point is min(n,(n+kd)/2), and nonexistent columns are not read even with an excess declared width. The difference that the reference PBSTF reads out-of-range columns when m>n is documented as a separate reproducer. C ABI is a separate task. |

Processes only the stored interval with native LLT, triangular solves, and selected-triangle rank updates, without full dense expansion. PBTRF/PBTF2 share the unblocked composition and do not claim a dedicated blocked band kernel. Verifies upper/lower storage, real diagonals, the non-positive-definite INFO, and solves interoperating with external factors. Allocates an O(min(kd,n)) vector snapshot internally. C ABI is a separate task. |

Verifies upper/lower storage, real diagonals, the non-positive-definite INFO, and solves interoperating with external factors. Allocates an O(min(kd,n)) vector snapshot internally. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `ptsv` / Fortran | `cptsv_`, `dptsv_`, `sptsv_`, `zptsv_` | [native LDL panels / unit band triangular_solve / real matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/cholesky/ldlt/factor.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Verifies reuse of external factors, non-positive-definite INFO and partial output, tiny/huge values, empty/zero RHS, and mixed ABIs. The reference complex PTTS2 rewrite for a zero RHS and its N=1 reciprocal overflow are documented as separate reproducers; the native implementation avoids touching unused arrays and keeps finite solutions through normalization. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `sbgst` / Fortran | `dsbgst_`, `ssbgst_` | [factorized triangular/split B + native triangular_solve / matmul / reflectors](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/triangular_solve.rs) | Connects eight Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Packed GST reads an external triangular Cholesky factor and applies the congruence transformation for ITYPE=1/2/3. Band GST reads the PBSTF split factor S and, through native block triangular solves and reflector composition, forms C=X^H A X and X^H B X=I within the original bandwidth. Preserves the unspecified triangle, padding, and BB, and reads only the real part of the complex Hermitian diagonal. Allocates dense internal matrices and does not claim the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. Band native rejections use the saved ILP64 driver. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `sbgv` / Fortran | `dsbgv_`, `ssbgv_` | [native LLT / split-factor solves / self_adjoint_evd](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects 24 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Packed input stores a triangular Cholesky factor and band input the PBSTF split factor; composes the congruence transformation, native full EVD, and B normalization. GVD also uses the common native EVD and does not claim the identically named divide-and-conquer algorithm. GVX selects A/I/V; the band full-basis Q returns generalized eigenvectors, and AB returns the diagonal tridiagonal form. Positive ABSTOL, native failures, and precision-guarded range endpoints are delegated to the saved original ILP64 driver, converting only written LP64 IWORK/IFAIL slots. Allocates an O(n^2) owned matrix and scratch. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `sbmv` / Fortran | `csbmv_`, `dsbmv_`, `ssbmv_`, `zsbmv_` | [native interval matmul / packed outer products / triangular_solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Connects 44 Fortran BLAS symbols to both ABIs. Passes only the stored band/packed interval to native multiplication and distinguishes negative strides, complex symmetric versus Hermitian, real diagonals, and the unit diagonal. Triangular solves compose the interval's native dot product and a diagonal solve, and zero columns in the no-transpose case are skipped as in the reference Netlib. No full dense expansion; allocates internal storage for the X snapshot of the product and the necessary row gathers. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `sbtrd` / Fortran | `dsbtrd_`, `ssbtrd_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `sfrk` / Fortran | `dsfrk_`, `ssfrk_` | [RFP selected-triangle conversion / native triangular::matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/triangular.rs) | Connects the four Fortran entry points SSFRK/DSFRK/CHFRK/ZHFRK to both ABIs. Converts RFP odd/even, UPLO, real N/T versus complex N/C, and rank-k transpose. Allocates an O(n^2) internal native matrix. beta=0 uses the old C, and a zero product does not read A. The Hermitian diagonal reads only the real part, and the output imaginary part is zero. This is not a classification asserting that dedicated native RFP APIs of the same name exist. |

| `sptrd` / Fortran | `dsptrd_`, `ssptrd_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `trevc` / Fortran | `ctrevc_`, `dtrevc_`, `strevc_`, `ztrevc_` | [eigenvectors_from_schur_real/cplx + reverse-adjoint left vectors](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects eight Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. The right side uses the public Schur eigenvector helper, and the left side is generated from a reversed transpose/adjoint Schur form and then restored to the original column order. Converts SIDE L/R/B, HOWMNY A/B/S, normalization of real 2x2 pairs in SELECT and M/MM, back-transformation, packing of real conjugate pairs, and max-component normalization. TREVC3 shares the same native helper and matrix products and does not claim its own blocked back-substitution algorithm. The query returns the minimum WORK/RWORK of this composed path, and internally owned matrices/scratch are O(n^2). On native rejection it delegates to the saved original ILP64 TREVC/TREVC3, converting LP64 SELECT/M/INFO. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `ung2r` / Fortran | `cung2r_`, `zung2r_` | [qr_in_place / Householder / triangular solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Connects 36 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Provides A/TAU coefficient conversion, Q formation and left/right application, and GEQRS. The complex ordinary and nonnegative-diagonal variants generate LAPACK-gauge reflectors and preserve real beta. Allocates internal arrays, and no guarantee is made that each entry point uses the same Netlib implementation as its algorithm name. |
| `ungbr` / Fortran | `cungbr_`, `zungbr_` | [native bidiag panels / LAPACK reflector gauge / native GEMV updates](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/svd/bidiag.rs) | Connects 20 Fortran symbols to both ABIs. Normalizes each active column/row, uses the single-column panel of native bidiag_in_place, and converts complex beta and the reciprocal-coefficient convention to LAPACK's real D/E, complex TAUQ/TAUP, and stored vectors. GEBRD/GEBD2 share the unblocked composition. LABRD updates its owned working matrix in sequence, preserving caller A22 while returning the first NB rows/columns and the deferred X/Y. Q/P are generated and applied left/right from the same storage form with native GEMV and rank-one updates. Handles the minimum length and query of caller WORK but allocates internal storage separately. Verifies four types, both ABIs, upper/lower bidiagonal form, rectangular shapes, and interoperability with external factors. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `upgtr` / Fortran | `cupgtr_`, `zupgtr_` | [native tridiag / Householder generation and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/tridiag.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. SPTRD/HPTRD expand packed storage into an owned dense matrix and convert the real beta gauge of the existing native tridiagonalization to LAPACK AP/TAU form. OPGTR/UPGTR/OPMTR/UPMTR read only the valid tail of the packed reflector and share native Householder application. Zero coefficients in the tail, diagonal, and tridiagonal entries are not referenced when forming or applying Q. SBTRD/HBTRD expand the band and compose the same reduction, returning D/E with Q for VECT=V and X*Q for VECT=U. VECT=N does not reference Q. Complex band E is phase-adjusted to be nonnegative. Verifies fixed WORK, INFO, and storage guards. Internally owned matrices are O(n^2); no claim of the same performance as a dedicated band bulge chase. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `amax` / Fortran | `damax_`, `samax_` | [MatRef::min/max/sum + real-precision abs1 and first-index selection](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/mat/matref.rs) | Fourteen Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. Real MAX/MIN/SUM borrow strided vectors; absolute extrema and IAMIN allocate real comparison values and use native min/max. Complex IAMIN compares abs(real)+abs(imag), rounded in the real input precision, and returns the first 1-based logical index. Negative strides return zero without reading X; zero stride returns the first (absolute) value for extrema and zero for SUM/IAMIN. |

| `gees` / Fortran | `cgees_`, `dgees_`, `sgees_`, `zgees_` | [hessenberg_schur_real/cplx / reorder_schur_real/cplx / Householder](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects 20 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Composes the native Schur factorization of the active block and the basis change, updates the existing Z, and handles the GEES SELECT callback, real conjugate pairs, SDIM, and reordering. HSEQR/LAHQR/LAQR0/LAQR4 share the native path and do not reproduce Netlib's per-entry QR algorithm selection. Allocates internal arrays; on non-convergence, because the public API returns no partial indices, reports the whole active range as non-converged with INFO=IHI. |

| `gegs` / Fortran | `cgegs_`, `dgegs_`, `sgegs_`, `zgegs_` | [QR / generalized_hessenberg / hessenberg_to_qz / generalized reorder_schur](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/gevd/qz_real/mod.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Converts the HGEQZ active range and Q/Z=N/I/V, the dense reduction of GGES/GGES3/GEGS, homogeneous alpha/beta, SELECT and real conjugate pairs, and SDIM. Preserves beta=0. Because the native API does not return a non-convergence state, the structure and outputs are validated, and an incomplete result reports the whole active range as non-converged with INFO=IHI. Re-evaluates QZ after reordering. Allocates internal arrays and scratch and provides the minimum legal WORK and query for each entry point. |

| `gemqr` / Fortran | `cgemqr_`, `dgemqr_`, `sgemqr_`, `zgemqr_` | [native compact-WY QR panels / Householder block application / modified LU with native matmul and triangular solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Twenty-eight Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. LATSQR stores separate T blocks for the initial row panel and each subsequent [R;B] panel, preserving earlier reflectors. TSQR Q generation/application gathers the correct noncontiguous row/column blocks. GEQR implements the five-entry header, optimal/minimal TSIZE/LWORK queries and compact fallback; GEMQR reads external GEQR metadata. ORHR_COL/UNHR_COL reconstruct Q through modified LU of Q-[D;0], native trailing updates and native triangular solves. GETSQRHRT composes TSQR, explicit thin Q, reconstruction and D*R. Internal temporary storage is owned by the adapter; ORGTSQR_ROW uses the common explicit-Q path and does not claim the reference in-place row algorithm. C API is separate. |

| `geql2` / Fortran | `cgeql2_`, `dgeql2_`, `sgeql2_`, `zgeql2_` | [reversed native-backed QR / adjoint QR LQ / prefix Householder application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Forty-eight Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. Both axes are reversed around existing standard QR/LQ, then triangular placement and TAU order are reversed back. QL uses H(k)...H(1); complex RQ uses H(1)^H...H(k)^H with conjugated row-vector storage. Thin Q uses the last columns (QL) or rows (RQ). Generation snapshots only stored reflector tails, and application skips zero coefficients and validates only active prefix extents. Blocked/unblocked names share an unblocked composition and own temporary storage; they do not claim dedicated native QL/RQ APIs or reference blocking performance. C API is separate. |

| `getsqrhrt` / Fortran | `cgetsqrhrt_`, `dgetsqrhrt_`, `sgetsqrhrt_`, `zgetsqrhrt_` | [native compact-WY QR panels / Householder block application / modified LU with native matmul and triangular solve](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Twenty-eight Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. TSQR Q generation/application gathers the correct noncontiguous row/column blocks. GEQR implements the five-entry header, optimal/minimal TSIZE/LWORK queries and compact fallback; GEMQR reads external GEQR metadata. ORHR_COL/UNHR_COL reconstruct Q through modified LU of Q-[D;0], native trailing updates and native triangular solves. GETSQRHRT composes TSQR, explicit thin Q, reconstruction and D*R. Internal temporary storage is owned by the adapter; ORGTSQR_ROW uses the common explicit-Q path and does not claim the reference in-place row algorithm. C API is separate. |

| `gges` / Fortran | `cgges_`, `dgges_`, `sgges_`, `zgges_` | [QR / generalized_hessenberg / hessenberg_to_qz / generalized reorder_schur](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/gevd/qz_real/mod.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Converts the HGEQZ active range and Q/Z=N/I/V, the dense reduction of GGES/GGES3/GEGS, homogeneous alpha/beta, SELECT and real conjugate pairs, and SDIM. Preserves beta=0. Because the native API does not return a non-convergence state, the structure and outputs are validated, and an incomplete result reports the whole active range as non-converged with INFO=IHI. Re-evaluates QZ after reordering. Allocates internal arrays and scratch and provides the minimum legal WORK and query for each entry point. |

Internal temporary storage is owned by the adapter; TRD accepts minimum LWORK=1, without claiming the reference blocked algorithm. C API is tracked separately. |

| `hgeqz` / Fortran | `chgeqz_`, `dhgeqz_`, `shgeqz_`, `zhgeqz_` | [QR / generalized_hessenberg / hessenberg_to_qz / generalized reorder_schur](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/gevd/qz_real/mod.rs) | Connects 16 Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. Because the native API does not return a non-convergence state, the structure and outputs are validated, and an incomplete result reports the whole active range as non-converged with INFO=IHI. Complex M/1/I use modulus, not native component-max or abs1; F/E retain components and normalize before native l2 to avoid the pinned extreme-range defect. Unit diagonals and ignored Hermitian imaginary components are not read. RFP N/T/C and NaN/Inf behavior are tested. Temporary storage is allocated internally; caller WORK is preserved. |

| `orm22` / Fortran | `dorm22_`, `sorm22_` | [native rectangular / triangular block matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Connects the four Fortran symbols of ORM22/UNM22 to both ABIs. Distinguishes the row partition (N1,N2) and column partition (N2,N1) of Q and applies the lower part of Q12 and the upper part of Q21 with native triangular matmul. Does not read the unspecified triangle or leading-dimension padding. Handles left/right, real N/T, complex N/C, and empty blocks, allocating an O(m*n) internal result in the same form as C and copying it back at the end. The query is m*n, and the minimum work for the actual computation is NQ as in the reference execution code (1 for empty blocks). Even at the minimum length, native internal storage is needed separately. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `ormql` / Fortran | `dormql_`, `sormql_` | [reversed native-backed QR / adjoint QR LQ / prefix Householder application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/qr/no_pivoting/factor.rs) | Forty-eight Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. Both axes are reversed around existing standard QR/LQ, then triangular placement and TAU order are reversed back. QL uses H(k)...H(1); complex RQ uses H(1)^H...H(k)^H with conjugated row-vector storage. Thin Q uses the last columns (QL) or rows (RQ). Generation snapshots only stored reflector tails, and application skips zero coefficients and validates only active prefix extents. Blocked/unblocked names share an unblocked composition and own temporary storage; they do not claim dedicated native QL/RQ APIs or reference blocking performance. C API is separate. |

| `pstf2` / Fortran | `cpstf2_`, `dpstf2_`, `spstf2_`, `zpstf2_` | [single-pivot native LLT / native GEMM](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/cholesky/llt_pivoting/factor.rs) | Eight Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs are connected. The adapter controls pivoting together with LAPACK TOL/INFO/PIV and composes a native LLT and a native GEMM Schur update for each pivot. The automatic tolerance is N*DLAMCH(E)*largest diagonal. It stops at TOL after the first positive pivot and returns the completed factors, the uncomputed permuted triangle, and the stopping diagonal. PSTRF/PSTF2 share the sequential path and allocate internal matrices and scratch. |

| `tplqt` / Fortran | `ctplqt_`, `dtplqt_`, `stplqt_`, `ztplqt_` | [native-backed QR of triangular-pentagonal stacks / compact Householder factor and application](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/householder.rs) | Twenty-eight Fortran symbols connected for both integer ABIs. Blocked names compose native-backed operations with owned temporary storage; they do not claim dedicated triangular-pentagonal kernels or reference blocking performance. No entry has a workspace query; factor2 has no WORK argument. C API remains separate. |

TSQR Q generation/application gathers the correct noncontiguous row/column blocks. GEQR implements the five-entry header, optimal/minimal TSIZE/LWORK queries and compact fallback; GEMQR reads external GEQR metadata. ORHR_COL/UNHR_COL reconstruct Q through modified LU of Q-[D;0], native trailing updates and native triangular solves. GETSQRHRT composes TSQR, explicit thin Q, reconstruction and D*R. Internal temporary storage is owned by the adapter; ORGTSQR_ROW uses the common explicit-Q path and does not claim the reference in-place row algorithm. C API is separate. |

C API is separate.; C ABI connected for LP64/ILP64; #288 tests explicit layout/char/integer/INFO contracts and DLL/LBT routes; NaN policy helpers retain C-int flags in both interfaces |
| `unm22` / Fortran | `cunm22_`, `zunm22_` | [native rectangular / triangular block matmul](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/matmul/mod.rs) | Connects the four Fortran symbols of ORM22/UNM22 to both ABIs. Distinguishes the row partition (N1,N2) and column partition (N2,N1) of Q and applies the lower part of Q12 and the upper part of Q21 with native triangular matmul. Does not read the unspecified triangle or leading-dimension padding. Handles left/right, real N/T, complex N/C, and empty blocks, allocating an O(m*n) internal result in the same form as C and copying it back at the end. The query is m*n, and the minimum work for the actual computation is NQ as in the reference execution code (1 for empty blocks). Even at the minimum length, native internal storage is needed separately. C ABI is a separate task. |

QL uses H(k)...H(1); complex RQ uses H(1)^H...H(k)^H with conjugated row-vector storage. Thin Q uses the last columns (QL) or rows (RQ). Generation snapshots only stored reflector tails, and application skips zero coefficients and validates only active prefix extents. TRSEN JOB=N performs stable selection with native Schur block moves, plus M, eigenvalue extraction, and Q updates. GEESX SENSE=N uses the existing native GEES path. No public native API exists for S/SEP or RCONDE/RCONDV, so JOB/SENSE E/V/B are delegated to the saved original ILP64 routines. For LP64, only written IWORK/BWORK slots are converted, and the SELECT callback converts the LOGICAL ABI with a thread-local guard, restoring it after nested calls. TRSEN handles only LWORK=-1, and real GEESX performs the collective LWORK/LIWORK query. Allocates internally owned matrices and scratch. C ABI is a separate task. |

| `trsen` / Fortran | `ctrsen_`, `dtrsen_`, `strsen_`, `ztrsen_` | [Schur and reorder_schur](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/evd/mod.rs) | Connects eight Fortran symbols, four types, and both ABIs. TRSEN JOB=N performs stable selection with native Schur block moves, plus M, eigenvalue extraction, and Q updates. GEESX SENSE=N uses the existing native GEES path. On rejected swaps, SDIM returns the total number of selected columns rather than the moved prefix (the common GEES handling is fixed too). No public native API exists for S/SEP or RCONDE/RCONDV, so JOB/SENSE E/V/B are delegated to the saved original ILP64 routines. For LP64, only written IWORK/BWORK slots are converted, and the SELECT callback converts the LOGICAL ABI with a thread-local guard, restoring it after nested calls. TRSEN handles only LWORK=-1, and real GEESX performs the collective LWORK/LIWORK query. Allocates internally owned matrices and scratch. C ABI is a separate task. |

### No independent public API (within reviewed scope)

| Family / ABI | All symbols | Public feature and evidence | Contract differences |
|---|---|---|---|
| `spsv` / Fortran | `cspsv_`, `zspsv_` | [—](https://github.com/tensor4all/colmat-rs/blob/4e6ed0e34659147a5c744ab6431f150e78db6f82/faer/src/linalg/cholesky/bunch_kaufman/mod.rs) | Scope of review: the public Bunch-Kaufman factor/solve has a complex Hermitian LDL^H contract. It differs from the SY/SP family that passes complex symmetric LDL^T and its IPIV/factors. Does not conclude that the same compact factor contract can be obtained from a general LU. |

## Direct ABI execution evidence (#317, #271)

`direct_call_witness` covers 2,912 canonical entries, with LP64 and ILP64 recorded separately.
The [ABI evidence snapshot](../assets/lapack-abi-execution.json) identifies the exact source/native revisions, manifest and test source SHA-256 hashes. Stale evidence is rejected.
CBLAS records completed named DLL/LBT calls explicitly: enums stay Cint, dimensions/strides use the selected integer ABI, and ROTG has no integer arguments.
LARTG/LARTGP/LARTGS have no integer arguments; their entry names still distinguish the two LBT tables. Shared native addresses do not identify individual aliases; named LBT stubs provide that evidence.
The collector observes project-owned pointer ccalls. High-level standard-library calls, isolated child processes and static symbol/library ccalls are excluded. Consult the individual source/signature/route witnesses and the numerical tests together.

```sh
julia --project -e 'using Pkg; Pkg.test(; allow_reresolve=false)'
python3 docs/import_abi_execution.py test-output/abi-execution.toml
```

Linux/macOS CI collects the same evidence during the ordinary full suite and saves separate ABI artifacts.

## Original candidate set and the 51 re-reviews

Retains the history of the 2,910 candidates and 51 items from the original #262 snapshot (`3c850a88a05b3c09eadd84d3722b1704cf1421f1` / native `e1c83482f5a61449d30e989808c3ba5f751ec391`).
[The all-canonical per-owner re-review TSV](../assets/lbt-candidate-coverage.tsv) and [the JSON of audit conditions and original Issue body hashes](../assets/lbt-candidate-audit.json) are recorded separately.
Fortran/CBLAS are mapped to the original explicit lists, and LAPACKE to the shared ABI scope of #288 and the numerical Issues of the Fortran counterparts. The numerical counterparts of the 148 LAPACKE candidates belong to #262, which was connected at the baseline. The ownership of the shared ABI and the registration of numerical counterparts are not proof that all contracts are implemented or verified.
Adds the 40 candidates (20 Fortran and 20 LAPACKE) that overlooked public rook pivoting, tracked in [Issue #372](https://github.com/AtelierArith/Colmat.jl/issues/372). This is a classification correction because public enums/fields also exist in the original baseline, not the addition of a new native API. With the correction, the original candidate set contains 2,950 entries.
No symbols were removed from the original positive API determinations. Of the old unsupported classifications, 1,918 were separated as unverified and are not dropped from the candidates as absent independent public APIs. They are not judged to be numerically implemented or confirmed as additional features.

### Separate columns for classification and evidence

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `capability_status` / `colmat_api` | Classifies semantic correspondence of public numerical features, storage format, partial support, and unverified status |
| `capability_evidence` / `evidence_status` | Verified revision, types, storage, options, verification method, public source, and reason. Absence determinations with insufficient records are not adopted |
| `adapter_lp64` / `adapter_ilp64` | Measured current LBT registrations. Separate from whether a public API exists |
| `execution_evidence_status` / `execution_evidence` | `direct_call_witness` records a completed concrete call through a DLL or LBT entry. It does not certify every option, numerical contract or independent algorithm. `not_catalogued` does not mean untested. |
| `independent_algorithm_status` | Verification status of a dedicated independent algorithm. Currently `not_assessed`. Do not infer a dedicated algorithm from the existence of a basic solver or API |

## Regeneration and completeness checks

The public API assessments are kept in `docs/lbt-capabilities.json`, and the actual symbol set is obtained from LBT. Routines with no registered rule and old unsupported determinations with insufficient evidence are retained as "Unverified; insufficient evidence". Suffixes/prefixes are not evidence of absence.

```sh
python3 docs/generate_lbt_inventory.py
julia --project=test test/lbt_inventory.jl
python3 -m unittest discover -s docs/tests -v
```

To regenerate the candidate re-review, pass the TSV of the pinned snapshot (`git show 3c850a88a05b3c09eadd84d3722b1704cf1421f1:docs/src/assets/lbt-symbol-inventory.tsv`) and a JSON containing the original 51 Issue bodies to `python3 docs/audit_lbt_candidates.py --baseline-tsv ORIGINAL.tsv --issue-snapshot docs/audits/issue-263-scope-snapshot.json --rook-issue 372`. The output records body hashes and retrieval times, and does not confuse past bodies with the current snapshot.

This audit is a public API mapping of a pinned source. If public features are added later, re-read the classification rules and restrictions and update it.
